# Inputs. Resources are pointers only: R = (ID3D12Resource *)0x1000, R2 = 0x2000 (printed R, R2, null). Truth table:
#   type global|texture|buffer, SyncBefore from {NONE=0, ALL=1, COPY=0x200, SPLIT=0x80000000}, SyncAfter ALL, AccessBefore and
#   AccessAfter each from the 29 named values below (the header's single accesses, combinations, 0x800000, 0x7fffffff);
#   texture: LayoutBefore = LayoutAfter = COMMON, pResource = R, Subresources and Flags zero; buffer: pResource = R, Offset 0, Size 256.
#   texture-layouts rows: sync RENDER_TARGET -> PIXEL_SHADING, access RENDER_TARGET -> SHADER_RESOURCE, layouts from
#   {UNDEFINED, COMMON, 2, 6, 0x15} x {UNDEFINED, COMMON, 3, 8, 0x1b}, Flags {NONE, DISCARD}, Subresources {lb&3,2,1,3,0,2}.
#   groups rows (SyncAfter ALL throughout): global[0] = sync ALL, UAV -> SHADER_RESOURCE; global[1] = sync NONE, UAV -> SHADER_RESOURCE;
#     global[2] = sync ALL, RENDER_TARGET -> COPY_SOURCE; texture = sync RENDER_TARGET -> PIXEL_SHADING, RENDER_TARGET -> SHADER_RESOURCE,
#     layouts RENDER_TARGET -> SHADER_RESOURCE, R2; buffer[0] = sync ALL, UAV -> UAV, R, 0, 64; buffer[1] = sync ALL, NO_ACCESS ->
#     COPY_DEST, R2, 0, 64. groups[] = {BUFFER x2 (buffer[]), GLOBAL x3 (global[]), TEXTURE x1, type 99 x2}. `groups four`:
#     LegacyBarriers(4, groups, out) with out holding 5 entries; `first three`: count 3, out holding 3; `null array`: (3, nullptr,
#     out of 2); `count 0`: (0, groups, out of 2); `null barrier arrays`: {BUFFER x2, GLOBAL x2, TEXTURE x2 with null arrays, BUFFER x0}
#     out of 1; `unknown group type 7`: one group {7, 3 barriers}.
#   Output: n=<barriers> then T(resource,sub=<subresource>,0x<before>-><after>) transitions, U(resource) UAV barriers,
#   A(before,after) aliasing barriers (the "any resource" barrier is A(null,null)).
#   legacy-state / initial-state rows: LegacyState(access) over the 29 values and every single bit; InitialState(layout, heap)
#   over the header's layouts, 0..0x20 and 0xffffffff, 0x7fffffff, 0x1000, heap types 0..4.
